# SEAM CONTRACT - unified CRM model (phases 1b and 1c)

This file is AUTHORITATIVE for the names used by the phase 2, 3 and 4 planners and executors.
If a plan disagrees with it, this file wins.
It was verified by running the migrations, the schema and the service code on a scratch database; every name below exists in the plans `05a`, `05b` and `06`.
Decisions and alternatives are in `nexo/knowledge/decisions/2026-10-21-crm-unificado-modelo.md`.

## Slices and files

| Slice | Plan | Creates |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 05a | `05a-crm-schema-migracao.md` | ADR, Drizzle tables in `apps/api/src/db/schema.ts`, migration `0029_crm_unificado_modelo` (SQL, snapshot, journal), tests `src/db/__tests__/crm-schema.test.ts` and `test/rls/crm-schema-rls.test.ts` |
| 05b | `05b-crm-defaults-servicos.md` | `packages/shared-utils/src/crm-model.ts` (subpath `@fxl-sales/shared-utils/crm-model`), `apps/api/src/domains/crm/defaults.ts`, tests |
| 06 | `06-migracao-dados-crm.md` | migration `0030_crm_legacy_copy` (data only), the migration oracle `test/rls/crm-legacy-copy-migration.test.ts`, seed delete order, parity hook |

Migration file names (journal tags): `0029_crm_unificado_modelo`, `0030_crm_legacy_copy`.
The next free migration number is `0031`.
Both migrations are ordinary (non-phased), 100% additive, and carry no GRANT or ROLE.

## Rules every later slice must keep

- The old tables `sales_ops_leads`, `sales_ops_lead_products`, `sales_ops_lead_stages` are never altered. The phase 2 API cutover is code only.
- A phase 2 cutover migration (`0031_*`) repeats the `0030_crm_legacy_copy.sql` statements and then RESYNCS rows changed in the old tables since 0030 (UPDATE changed leads, upsert changed values, one history event per changed stage) BEFORE the reads switch. Generate it with `drizzle-kit generate --custom` and copy the file body.
- Every new org-scoped table: `org_id text NOT NULL`, composite FKs with `org_id` first onto a `UNIQUE (org_id, id)`, FORCE RLS with `<table>_tenant_isolation` and `<table>_admin_context` spelled like 0022.
- An enumeration that may grow gets NO CHECK (the guard forbids DROP CONSTRAINT); validate it with zod and the constants below.
- drizzle-kit emits FKs before the unique indexes they need when the tables are new: reorder to CREATE TABLE, indexes, FKs (the slice 05a helper script does it).
- Writers of `stage_id` or `position` on the NEW lead table take the per-org advisory lock the old `lockLeadBoard` takes (same key, same order), right after the scope gate and before any row lock. Phase 2 owns that function.
- Only `ensureDefaultPipeline`, `ensureSystemFieldDefinitions`, `ensureCrmDefaults`, `ensureCrmDefaultsForOrg` may seed; the allow-list test `apps/api/src/domains/crm/__tests__/crm-seed-callers.test.ts` (`ALLOWED_CALLERS`) is where phase 2 registers a request path.
- Civil days (`due_on`, `value_date`) are `date` columns in string mode, never `Date`.

## Tables (SQL name -> Drizzle export in `apps/api/src/db/schema.ts`)

| SQL table | Drizzle export | Row type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `sales_ops_pipelines` | `salesOpsPipelines` | `typeof salesOpsPipelines.$inferSelect` |
| `sales_ops_pipeline_stages` | `salesOpsPipelineStages` | idem |
| `sales_ops_pipeline_transitions` | `salesOpsPipelineTransitions` | idem |
| `sales_ops_field_definitions` | `salesOpsFieldDefinitions` | idem |
| `sales_ops_crm_leads` | `salesOpsCrmLeads` | idem |
| `sales_ops_crm_lead_field_values` | `salesOpsCrmLeadFieldValues` | idem |
| `sales_ops_crm_lead_events` | `salesOpsCrmLeadEvents` | idem |
| `sales_ops_crm_lead_notes` | `salesOpsCrmLeadNotes` | idem |
| `sales_ops_crm_lead_followups` | `salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups` | idem |
| `sales_ops_org_modules` | `salesOpsOrgModules` | idem |

Drizzle property names are the camelCase of the SQL column. All tables have `id uuid PK default gen_random_uuid()`, `org_id text NOT NULL`, `created_at timestamptz NOT NULL default now()`; the mutable ones have `updated_at timestamptz NULL` (set by the service, no trigger).

### `sales_ops_pipelines`
`name text NOT NULL`, `system_key text NULL` (`'crm-padrao'` for the seeded one), `position int NOT NULL default 0`, `is_default bool NOT NULL default false`, `status text NOT NULL default 'active'` (CHECK `active|archived`), `archived_at timestamptz NULL`.
Indexes: unique `(org_id, id)`, unique `(org_id, name)`, unique partial `(org_id, system_key) WHERE system_key IS NOT NULL`, unique partial `(org_id) WHERE is_default` (at most one default per org).

### `sales_ops_pipeline_stages`
`pipeline_id uuid NOT NULL`, `name text NOT NULL`, `position int NOT NULL default 0`, `type text NOT NULL default 'normal'` (CHECK `normal|orcamento|saida`), `behavior text NOT NULL default 'none'` (no CHECK; `none|opens_orcamentos`), `exit_kind text NULL` (CHECK `ganho|perdido|pausado`), `requires_reason bool NOT NULL default false`, `status`, `archived_at`.
CHECKs: `(type = 'saida') = (exit_kind IS NOT NULL)`; `NOT requires_reason OR exit_kind = 'perdido'`.
Indexes: unique `(org_id, id)`, unique `(org_id, pipeline_id, id)` (FK target for leads and transitions), unique `(org_id, pipeline_id, name)`, `(org_id, pipeline_id, position)`.
FK `(org_id, pipeline_id) -> pipelines (org_id, id)` restrict.
Migrated legacy stages keep their old `sales_ops_lead_stages.id`.

### `sales_ops_pipeline_transitions`
`pipeline_id`, `from_stage_id`, `to_stage_id` (all uuid NOT NULL).
Unique `(org_id, from_stage_id, to_stage_id)`; CHECK `from_stage_id <> to_stage_id`; FKs `(org_id, pipeline_id, from_stage_id)` and `(org_id, pipeline_id, to_stage_id)` -> stages `(org_id, pipeline_id, id)` restrict.
A pair that is absent is not allowed.

### `sales_ops_field_definitions`
`key text NOT NULL` (unique per org), `label text NOT NULL`, `kind text NOT NULL default 'text'` (no CHECK; `text|number|money|date|options|reference`), `reference_target text NULL` (no CHECK; `cliente|contato|produto|servico|venda`, set exactly when `kind = 'reference'`, CHECK), `multiple bool default false`, `options jsonb NULL` (`[{key, label}]`), `filterable bool default false`, `is_system bool default false`, `removable bool default true` (CHECK `removable OR is_system`), `backing_column text NULL` (`'client_id'` for Cliente), `position int`, `status`, `archived_at`.
The Cliente row: `key 'cliente'`, `label 'Cliente'`, `kind 'reference'`, `reference_target 'cliente'`, `multiple false`, `filterable true`, `is_system true`, `removable false`, `backing_column 'client_id'`, `position 1`. Constant: `SYSTEM_FIELD_CLIENTE`.
Backfilled optional fields (only when data exists): `produtos` (reference produto, multiple, position 2), `descricao` (text, position 3), `nascimento` (date, position 4).

### `sales_ops_crm_leads`
`contact_name text NOT NULL`, `contact_phone`, `contact_email` (text NULL), `pipeline_id uuid NOT NULL`, `stage_id uuid NOT NULL`, `position int default 0`, `stage_changed_at timestamptz NOT NULL default now()`, `seller_person_id uuid NULL`, `seller_name_snapshot text NOT NULL default ''`, `client_id uuid NULL`, `client_name_snapshot text NOT NULL default ''`, `estimated_value_brl int NOT NULL default 0` (CHECK `>= 0`), `lost_reason text NULL`, `sale_id uuid NULL`, `deleted_at`, `deleted_by_user_id`, `deleted_by_name` (CHECK `(deleted_at IS NULL) = (deleted_by_user_id IS NULL)`).
Indexes: unique `(org_id, id)`; `(org_id, stage_id, position)` (board column); `(org_id, pipeline_id)`; `(org_id, seller_person_id)`; `(org_id, created_at)` (period filter); unique partial `(org_id, sale_id) WHERE sale_id IS NOT NULL` (one lead per proposta, deleted included); partial `(org_id, deleted_at, id) WHERE deleted_at IS NOT NULL`.
FKs (all restrict, composite): pipeline `(org_id, pipeline_id)`, stage `(org_id, pipeline_id, stage_id)`, client, seller person, sale.
Dynamic fields are NOT columns here. Birth date, description and produtos are field values.

### `sales_ops_crm_lead_field_values` (EAV, typed)
`lead_id`, `field_id` (uuid NOT NULL), `position int NOT NULL default 0` (CHECK `>= 0`; 0 for single-valued), `value_text text`, `value_number numeric(20,6)`, `value_cents bigint` (Drizzle `mode: 'number'`), `value_date date` (string), `value_option text`, `value_client_id uuid`, `value_product_id uuid`, `value_sale_id uuid`, `ref_name_snapshot text`, `updated_at`.
CHECK `num_nonnulls(value_text, value_number, value_cents, value_date, value_option, value_client_id, value_product_id, value_sale_id) = 1`.
Unique `(org_id, lead_id, field_id, position)`; one partial btree per slot for filtering `(org_id, field_id, <slot>) WHERE <slot> IS NOT NULL` (client and sale slots: `(org_id, <slot>)`).
FKs: lead (cascade), field, client, product, sale (restrict).
Slot by field: text -> `value_text`; number -> `value_number`; money -> `value_cents` (integer cents); date -> `value_date`; options -> `value_option` (the option key); reference cliente -> `value_client_id` + snapshot; reference produto or servico -> `value_product_id` + snapshot, OR `value_text` + snapshot for a FREE item (no catalog row); reference venda -> `value_sale_id`; reference contato -> `value_text` (no entity yet).
Matching slot to definition is a zod rule in the phase 2 service.

### `sales_ops_crm_lead_events`
`lead_id`, `kind text NOT NULL default 'stage_changed'` (no CHECK), `from_stage_id uuid NULL`, `to_stage_id uuid NULL`, `reason text NULL`, `actor_user_id text NULL`, `actor_name_snapshot text NULL`, `occurred_at timestamptz NOT NULL default now()`.
Unique `(org_id, id)`; `(org_id, lead_id, occurred_at, id)`; unique partial `(org_id, lead_id) WHERE from_stage_id IS NULL AND kind = 'stage_changed'` (exactly one initial entry per lead).
FKs restrict: lead, from stage `(org_id, from_stage_id)`, to stage `(org_id, to_stage_id)`.
Migrated first event: `from_stage_id NULL`, `to_stage_id` = the lead's stage, `occurred_at` = `stage_changed_at`, `actor_user_id 'system'`, `actor_name_snapshot 'Migração'`, `reason` = `lost_reason`.
Phase 2 writes ONE event per stage change in the SAME transaction as the move, with the actor name snapshotted from the token.

### `sales_ops_crm_lead_notes`
`lead_id`, `event_id uuid NULL` (the stage event the note was written with), `body text NOT NULL` (CHECK not blank), `author_user_id`, `author_name_snapshot`. FKs restrict: lead, event.

### `sales_ops_crm_lead_followups`
`lead_id`, `next_step text NOT NULL` (CHECK not blank), `due_on date NOT NULL` (civil day, string mode), `owner_person_id uuid NULL`, `owner_name_snapshot`, `done_at timestamptz NULL`, `created_by_user_id`, `created_by_name`, `updated_at`.
Partial index `(org_id, due_on) WHERE done_at IS NULL`. FKs restrict: lead, owner pessoa.

### `sales_ops_org_modules`
`module_key text NOT NULL` (no CHECK), `enabled bool NOT NULL default false`, `source text NOT NULL default 'default'` (no CHECK; `default|token_backfill|admin`), `updated_at`. Unique `(org_id, module_key)`.
Written by nobody before phase 3.

## Constants and pure functions (`@fxl-sales/shared-utils/crm-model`, file `packages/shared-utils/src/crm-model.ts`)

Run `pnpm --filter @fxl-sales/shared-utils build` after editing; the API resolves the package through `dist`.

- `CRM_STATUSES`, `CrmStatus`
- `PIPELINE_STAGE_TYPES` (`normal|orcamento|saida`), `PIPELINE_EXIT_KINDS` (`ganho|perdido|pausado`) (types `PipelineStageType`, `PipelineExitKind` come from `@fxl-sales/shared-utils/default-pipeline`)
- `STAGE_BEHAVIORS`, `StageBehavior`
- `FIELD_KINDS`, `FieldKind`; `FIELD_REFERENCE_TARGETS`, `FieldReferenceTarget`
- `LEAD_EVENT_KINDS`, `LeadEventKind`
- `CRM_MODULE_KEYS` (`orcamentos|comissoes|financeiro|finders|catalogo|importacao|clientes`), `CrmModuleKey`, `isCrmModuleKey(value)`, `CRM_MODULE_SOURCES`, `CrmModuleSource`
- `CAPABILITY_MODULES: Record<SalesCapability, readonly CrmModuleKey[]>` and `modulesForEdition(edition): Record<CrmModuleKey, boolean>` (phase 3 token backfill)
- `defaultStageBehavior(stage)`, `defaultStageRequiresReason(stage)`
- `planPipelineTransitions(stages: readonly TransitionPlanStage[]): [string, string][]` (the 0030 rule in TypeScript; phase 2 may use it to regenerate a graph)
- `SYSTEM_FIELD_CLIENTE`, `DEFAULT_PIPELINE_STAGE_BY_KEY`
- Existing, unchanged: `DEFAULT_PIPELINE_STAGES`, `DEFAULT_PIPELINE_TRANSITIONS`, `DEFAULT_PIPELINE_KEY` (`'crm-padrao'`), `DEFAULT_PIPELINE_NAME` (`'Funil CRM padrão'`) from `@fxl-sales/shared-utils/default-pipeline`.

## Service functions (`apps/api/src/domains/crm/defaults.ts`)

```ts
type Db = ReturnType<typeof getDb>;
export type PipelineRow = typeof salesOpsPipelines.$inferSelect;
export type FieldDefinitionRow = typeof salesOpsFieldDefinitions.$inferSelect;

/** Seeds the default pipeline only when the org has NO pipeline row. Inside a tenant tx. Returns the org's pipelines ordered by (position, name). */
export function ensureDefaultPipeline(tx: Db, orgId: string): Promise<PipelineRow[]>;
/** Inserts the Cliente system field with ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING. Returns the org's fields ordered by (position, key). */
export function ensureSystemFieldDefinitions(tx: Db, orgId: string): Promise<FieldDefinitionRow[]>;
export function ensureCrmDefaults(tx: Db, orgId: string): Promise<{ pipelines: PipelineRow[]; fields: FieldDefinitionRow[] }>;
/** Opens withTenant (domains/sales-ops/leads/with-tenant.js) and calls ensureCrmDefaults. */
export function ensureCrmDefaultsForOrg(db: Db, orgId: string): Promise<{ pipelines: PipelineRow[]; fields: FieldDefinitionRow[] }>;
```

There is no `copyLegacyLeadsToCrm` function: the copy is the migration SQL (`0030_crm_legacy_copy.sql`), run once at deploy, and a phase 2 migration repeats it.

## Fixed numbers the tests pin

- Default pipeline: 7 stages, 22 transitions, 1 pipeline, 1 field (`cliente`).
- Legacy org shaped like FXL (Novo/Em negociação/Proposta/Perdido): 4 stages copied with their ids, plus `Ganho` (position 5) and `Pausado` (position 6), 3 open stages + Perdido + Ganho + Pausado, 16 transitions (an archived 5th stage is excluded and keeps its position).
- 20 foreign keys across the ten tables; 10 tables, 20 policies.

## What the phase 2 to 4 planners must NOT redo

- Do not add columns to the lead table for dynamic fields; add a field definition and value rows.
- Do not read `sales_ops_lead_stages.kind` in new code; read `type`, `exit_kind`, `behavior`, `requires_reason` on `sales_ops_pipeline_stages`.
- Do not write `fxl-sales.*` outbox events for a lead (P2 stays untouched); a lead move never touches the ledger.
- Do not import the legacy `leads` table into the CRM (P1).
# Unified CRM model: new tables, copy-with-same-uuid, default pipeline, typed dynamic values

**Date:** 2026-10-21
**Surfaced by:** run `20261021T011348Z-vendas-crm-unificado` (milestone v4.8.0), phases 1b and 1c
**Survey:** `nexo/runs/20261021T011348Z-vendas-crm-unificado/levantamento-entidades.md` (passed its separate Verify), sections 1, 3.1, 3.3 to 3.6, 3.12, 3.13, 4 and 5.

## Context

Today a lead lives in `sales_ops_leads` (survey 3.1), its columns are the stage of one fixed four-stage list per org in `sales_ops_lead_stages` (survey 3.3), and the "funnel" is only that list (survey 3.4).
Migration 0022 blocks the product the owner asked for: `sales_ops_lead_stages_org_kind_idx` allows one conversion and one lost stage per org, `sales_ops_lead_stages_kind_check` only knows `normal|conversion|lost`, `sales_ops_lead_stages_system_kind_check` ties `is_system` to `kind`, `sales_ops_lead_stages_org_name_idx` forbids the same stage name in two funnels (survey 5.1).
Widening any of them needs DROP CONSTRAINT or DROP INDEX, and every migration after 0028 is 100% additive (guard `apps/api/src/db/__tests__/additive-migrations-guard.test.ts`).
Eleven files read `sales_ops_lead_stages.kind` and eighteen read `stage_id` (survey 5.2 and 5.3), so the old API must keep working until phase 2.

## Decision

D1 - Lead identity.
The unified lead lives in NEW tables.
`sales_ops_leads`, `sales_ops_lead_products` and `sales_ops_lead_stages` stay byte-intact: no new column, index, policy or trigger.
Migration 0030 COPIES with the SAME uuid (new lead id = old lead id; a migrated stage id = the old stage id), so URLs, `sale_id` links and the copy idempotency (`INSERT ... SELECT ... ON CONFLICT (id) DO NOTHING`) are stable.
Phase 2 cuts the API over to the new tables; until then the old reads keep working.
Alternatives rejected: (a) evolve `sales_ops_leads` in place - needs DROP/ALTER CHECK and index changes, forbidden, and puts 18 readers at risk at once; (b) a view over both tables - no writes, no FK targets; (c) copy with NEW uuids and a mapping table - breaks stable URLs and makes the copy non-idempotent without extra state.

D2 - Never touched.
Legacy `leads` (P1), `fxl-sales.*` events (P2), payables, receivables and settlements (P3), `sales_ops_sales`, items and professionals (P4), the affiliate module (P5).
The new lead references a proposta only by `sale_id`, a composite FK to `sales_ops_sales (org_id, id)` like today, with the same partial unique index `(org_id, sale_id) WHERE sale_id IS NOT NULL`: at most ONE lead per proposta in the org, deleted leads included.
They stay as pendencias of the survey (section 4); a later run owns them.

D3 - Tables (all org-scoped, FORCE RLS with `tenant_isolation` and `admin_context` exactly as 0022, composite FKs with `org_id` first onto a `UNIQUE (org_id, id)`, no GRANT or ROLE):
`sales_ops_pipelines`, `sales_ops_pipeline_stages`, `sales_ops_pipeline_transitions`, `sales_ops_field_definitions`, `sales_ops_crm_leads`, `sales_ops_crm_lead_field_values`, `sales_ops_crm_lead_events`, `sales_ops_crm_lead_notes`, `sales_ops_crm_lead_followups`, `sales_ops_org_modules`.
The exact columns are in `nexo/plans/vendas-crm-unificado/contracts/SEAM-CONTRACT.md`.

The lead keeps a FIXED core of real columns: name, contact phone and e-mail, pipeline, stage, position, `stage_changed_at`, seller, `client_id` plus `client_name_snapshot`, `sale_id`, `lost_reason`, soft delete, timestamps, and `estimated_value_brl integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (>= 0)`.
`estimated_value_brl` stays a real column although the survey lists it as configurable: the board sums it in SQL (`summarizeLeadStages` groups by stage, survey 3.1) and the `>= 0` floor and the default must survive (Verify note 1).
`estimated_value_brl` is the only money field; it is NOT a dynamic field.

Dynamic-value storage: EAV with typed columns, NOT a JSONB column with a GIN index.
Why: (1) reference values (Cliente, Produto, Serviço, Venda) need real composite FKs, otherwise a deleted produto or a foreign-org id leaves a dangling reference no constraint sees, and the survey shows how much of this product depends on those FKs (3.5, 3.8); JSONB cannot carry a FK; (2) "typed and filterable": a number, money or date range filter, an `ORDER BY` and a `SUM` need native types and btree indexes, while GIN serves containment only and every range or sort over JSONB needs casts the planner cannot estimate; (3) a multi-valued field (Produto, Serviço) is natural rows, and it follows the `sales_ops_lead_products` precedent (child rows, name snapshot, nullable product id); (4) RLS and the composite `org_id` FKs apply per row.
Cost accepted: one join (or `EXISTS`) per filtered field and one row per value; mitigated by one partial btree per value slot.
The value table has one slot per type (`value_text`, `value_number numeric(20,6)`, `value_cents bigint`, `value_date`, `value_option`, `value_client_id`, `value_product_id`, `value_sale_id`) and a CHECK that exactly ONE slot is set.
A FREE produto (no catalog row, survey-confirmed in `lead-schemas.ts` and `lead-service.ts`) is stored as `value_text` plus `ref_name_snapshot` with `value_product_id` NULL; a catalog produto sets `value_product_id` plus the snapshot.
A Contato reference has no Contato entity yet (survey 3.6, open question), so it is `value_text` plus snapshot with no FK.
That the slot matches the field definition's kind is a zod rule in the service layer, the same way "Perdido requires a reason" is (0022 header): a CHECK cannot join another table.

Cliente: the Cliente system field is ONE definition row (`key 'cliente'`, `is_system true`, `removable false`, `backing_column 'client_id'`) whose value lives in the lead's `client_id` and `client_name_snapshot` columns, never in value rows.
The columns keep the composite FK to `sales_ops_clients` and the snapshot every reader needs (survey 3.5), and the definition row is what makes it a non-removable field in the UI.
CHECK `removable OR is_system` makes a removable system field unrepresentable.

Stages: `type` is `normal | orcamento | saida`, `behavior` is open-ended text (`none | opens_orcamentos`), `exit_kind` is `ganho | perdido | pausado` and set exactly when `type = 'saida'` (CHECK), `requires_reason` may be true only for `perdido`.
A name is unique per pipeline, not per org.
No unique index limits the number of exits: several exits per pipeline are the point.
A lead's stage must belong to the lead's own pipeline: the FK `(org_id, pipeline_id, stage_id)` targets `UNIQUE (org_id, pipeline_id, id)` on the stages; a transition has the same two-ended composite FK, so it can never cross pipelines.

Enumerations that may grow carry NO CHECK: stage `behavior`, field `kind`, `reference_target`, module `key`, module `source`, event `kind`.
The additive guard forbids DROP CONSTRAINT, so a CHECK could never be widened.
They are validated by zod and by `packages/shared-utils/src/crm-model.ts`.
Closed sets keep their CHECK: stage `type`, `exit_kind`, `status`.

History: `sales_ops_crm_lead_events` is the server-written stage history, in its own table, never in `audit_log` (survey 3.13: the hash chain serializes every writer on one row lock).
A `NULL` `from_stage_id` is the lead's initial entry; a partial unique index allows at most one initial entry per lead.
Notes (`sales_ops_crm_lead_notes`) and follow-ups (`sales_ops_crm_lead_followups`, `due_on` civil day NOT NULL, owner pessoa, `done_at`) are separate tables.
Events, notes and follow-ups reference their lead with `ON DELETE restrict` (history is never cascaded); only value rows cascade from the lead (the `sales_ops_lead_products` precedent: no independent identity, nothing references them).

D4 - Default pipeline.
`ensureDefaultPipeline(tx, orgId)` seeds `DEFAULT_PIPELINE_NAME` ('Funil CRM padrão', system key `crm-padrao`, `is_default true`) from `DEFAULT_PIPELINE_STAGES` and `DEFAULT_PIPELINE_TRANSITIONS` only when the org has NO pipeline row at all (a renamed or archived pipeline is never reinserted), with the same concurrency safety as `ensureLeadStages`: a bare `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` insert of the pipeline row that a concurrent first caller blocks on until the winner commits, children written only by the caller whose insert returned a row, a re-read at the end.
Migration 0030 builds the pipeline of an org that HAS old stages from its OWN stages: same ids, names, positions, `conversion` -> `orcamento` with behavior `opens_orcamentos`, `lost` -> `saida` `perdido` requiring a reason, `normal` -> `normal`, archived stays archived.
It ADDS the missing exits `Ganho` and `Pausado` with new ids at `max(position) + 1` and `+ 2` (`Ganho (saída)` if the org already named a normal etapa `Ganho`), only when the pipeline has no stage with that `exit_kind`.
It does NOT add `Qualificação`: that is an open stage the org names itself.
The transition graph is generated by one rule over the ACTIVE stages of a pipeline that has no transition yet: open = `normal|orcamento` ordered by `(position, name)`, exits = `saida`; `open[i] -> open[i+1]`, `open[i+1] -> open[i]`, every open -> every exit, every `pausado` exit -> every open.
For the pure default pipeline this equals `DEFAULT_PIPELINE_TRANSITIONS` (22 pairs); `planPipelineTransitions` in `crm-model.ts` is the same rule in TypeScript and a test compares both.
An org with no old stages gets the pure default pipeline.
The org universe is the orgs 0022 seeded (people, settings, sales) plus every org with an etapa, a lead or a cliente.

D5 - Modules.
`sales_ops_org_modules (org_id, module_key, enabled, source default|token_backfill|admin, updated_at)` is created in 1b and written by nobody until phase 3.
No row is backfilled from the token in a migration (the token is not in the database); phase 3 does the one-time token backfill at request time.
Keys: `orcamentos`, `comissoes`, `financeiro`, `finders`, `catalogo`, `importacao`, `clientes` (the current `clients` capability needs its own key, because the leads edition grants it alone).
Mapping from `packages/shared-utils/src/sales-edition.ts` (`CAPABILITY_MODULES`; a capability is granted when ALL its modules are on): `proposals -> orcamentos`, `commissions -> comissoes`, `catalog -> catalogo`, `clients -> clientes`, `import -> importacao`, `finders -> finders`, `history -> financeiro`, `leadFullFields -> catalogo`.
`modulesForEdition('full')` is all seven on; `modulesForEdition('leads')` is exactly `clientes` and `importacao`, which is today's Construbom.
No org plan table is created: no plan semantics exist yet and the modules cover the need.

## Consequences

- Between 0030 and the phase 2 cutover the old tables stay the write path, so rows written in that window are not copied by 0030 alone.
- The repeat in 0031 only adds rows that do not exist yet.
- A lead that already exists in the new table is NOT refreshed by a verbatim repeat.
- The phase 2 cutover must therefore either (a) make 0031 a resync: after the repeated inserts, `UPDATE sales_ops_crm_leads ... FROM sales_ops_leads` for stage_id, position, stage_changed_at, seller, client, value, lost_reason, sale_id and deleted_* where they differ, `INSERT ... ON CONFLICT (org_id, lead_id, field_id, position) DO UPDATE` for value rows, and a stage_changed event for each changed stage, with a test that mutates the old tables between 0030 and 0031; or (b) dual-write old and new tables from the first phase 2 deploy until the cutover.
- Choose and test one; a verbatim repeat is not enough.
- No `sales_ops_crm_lead_field_values` row is written for Cliente by design; the owner accepted the backing-column form (D3).
- The dev seed deletes the new tables first (`SEED_DELETE_ORDER`), because `sales_ops_crm_leads` restrict-references clientes, pessoas, propostas and produtos.
- No existing API route, schema or web file changes in 1b and 1c.
- Open questions stay with the owner (survey section 6): whether contact requires phone or e-mail, whether Contato becomes an entity, whether finder referrals appear in the CRM, whether Orçamento creates a proposta.

## Verify notes applied

1. `estimated_value_brl` keeps NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 and the `>= 0` CHECK.
2. Only row `amountCents`, `saleId`, `saleCode`, the client name snapshot and `deepLinkPath` reach the Finance event, so nothing here changes it.
3. `restoreLead` depends on `kind` transitively through `firstOpenLeadStage`; the old API is untouched until phase 2.
4. "Excluded from the synced set on the producer" is the producer side, unchanged.
5. `sales_ops_leads_org_seller_idx` is mirrored as `sales_ops_crm_leads_org_seller_idx`.

/bin/zsh -lc 'cat apps/api/drizzle/0030_crm_legacy_copy.sql apps/api/src/domains/crm/defaults.ts packages/shared-utils/src/crm-model.ts' in /Users/cauetpinciara/Documents/fxl/projects/06--PRODUCT--fxl-sales/.worktrees/20261021T011348Z-vendas-crm-unificado/run
 succeeded in 0ms:
-- 0030_crm_legacy_copy - the additive backfill of the unified CRM model (phase 1c).
--
-- COPIES today's etapas and leads into the 0029 tables and READS everything else.
-- Every statement is INSERT ... SELECT ... ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING: running the whole
-- file twice writes nothing the second time, and no source table is ever written.
-- The legacy `leads` table (finder referrals), the ledger, the outbox and
-- sales_ops_sales are not read at all except for the org universe.
--
-- SAME UUIDS: a copied lead keeps its id, a copied etapa keeps its id, so URLs,
-- sale_id links and the ON CONFLICT (id) idempotency are stable.
--
-- Org universe = the orgs 0022 seeded (people, settings, sales) plus every org that
-- already has an etapa, a lead or a cliente.
-- Every org gets its default pipeline 'Funil CRM padrão' (system_key 'crm-padrao'):
--   * with legacy etapas: built from the org's OWN etapas (conversion -> orcamento
--     opens_orcamentos, lost -> saida/perdido requiring a reason, normal -> normal,
--     archived stays archived) plus the missing exits Ganho and Pausado at
--     max(position)+1 and +2, and the transition graph generated by rule below;
--   * without legacy etapas: the pure default pipeline (parity with
--     DEFAULT_PIPELINE_STAGES, asserted by default-pipeline-parity.test.ts).
-- Transition rule over the ACTIVE etapas of a pipeline with no transition yet, open =
-- type normal|orcamento ordered by (position, name), exits = type saida:
--   open[i] -> open[i+1], open[i+1] -> open[i], every open -> every exit,
--   every exit with exit_kind pausado -> every open.
-- Fields are created only when data needs them (Cliente always).
-- Each lead gets ONE first history event (from NULL to its etapa, occurred_at =
-- stage_changed_at, actor 'system'/'Migração').
-- Modules are NOT backfilled here: the token is not in the database.
SELECT set_config('app.fxl_admin', 'true', true);--> statement-breakpoint
INSERT INTO "sales_ops_pipelines" ("org_id", "name", "system_key", "position", "is_default")
SELECT o."org_id", 'Funil CRM padrão', 'crm-padrao', 0, true
FROM (
  SELECT "org_id" FROM "sales_ops_people"
  UNION SELECT "org_id" FROM "sales_ops_settings"
  UNION SELECT "org_id" FROM "sales_ops_sales"
  UNION SELECT "org_id" FROM "sales_ops_lead_stages"
  UNION SELECT "org_id" FROM "sales_ops_leads"
  UNION SELECT "org_id" FROM "sales_ops_clients"
) AS o
ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;--> statement-breakpoint
INSERT INTO "sales_ops_pipeline_stages" ("id", "org_id", "pipeline_id", "name", "position", "type", "behavior", "exit_kind", "requires_reason", "status", "archived_at", "created_at", "updated_at")
SELECT s."id", s."org_id", p."id", s."name", s."position",
  CASE s."kind" WHEN 'conversion' THEN 'orcamento' WHEN 'lost' THEN 'saida' ELSE 'normal' END,
  CASE s."kind" WHEN 'conversion' THEN 'opens_orcamentos' ELSE 'none' END,
  CASE s."kind" WHEN 'lost' THEN 'perdido' ELSE NULL END,
  s."kind" = 'lost',
  s."status", s."archived_at", s."created_at", s."updated_at"
FROM "sales_ops_lead_stages" s
JOIN "sales_ops_pipelines" p ON p."org_id" = s."org_id" AND p."system_key" = 'crm-padrao'
ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;--> statement-breakpoint
INSERT INTO "sales_ops_pipeline_stages" ("org_id", "pipeline_id", "name", "position", "type", "behavior", "exit_kind", "requires_reason")
SELECT p."org_id", p."id", d."label", d."ord", d."type", d."behavior", d."exit_kind", d."requires_reason"
FROM "sales_ops_pipelines" p
CROSS JOIN (VALUES
  ('Novo',          1, 'normal',    'none',             NULL,       false),
  ('Qualificação',  2, 'normal',    'none',             NULL,       false),
  ('Em negociação', 3, 'normal',    'none',             NULL,       false),
  ('Proposta',      4, 'orcamento', 'opens_orcamentos', NULL,       false),
  ('Ganho',         5, 'saida',     'none',             'ganho',    false),
  ('Perdido',       6, 'saida',     'none',             'perdido',  true),
  ('Pausado',       7, 'saida',     'none',             'pausado',  false)
) AS d("label", "ord", "type", "behavior", "exit_kind", "requires_reason")
WHERE p."system_key" = 'crm-padrao'
  AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM "sales_ops_pipeline_stages" x WHERE x."pipeline_id" = p."id")
ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;--> statement-breakpoint
INSERT INTO "sales_ops_pipeline_stages" ("org_id", "pipeline_id", "name", "position", "type", "behavior", "exit_kind", "requires_reason")
SELECT p."org_id", p."id",
  CASE WHEN EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM "sales_ops_pipeline_stages" y WHERE y."pipeline_id" = p."id" AND y."name" = e."label")
       THEN e."label" || ' (saída)' ELSE e."label" END,
  (SELECT COALESCE(MAX(z."position"), 0) FROM "sales_ops_pipeline_stages" z WHERE z."pipeline_id" = p."id") + e."ord",
  'saida', 'none', e."exit_kind", false
FROM "sales_ops_pipelines" p
CROSS JOIN (VALUES ('Ganho', 'ganho', 1), ('Pausado', 'pausado', 2)) AS e("label", "exit_kind", "ord")
WHERE p."system_key" = 'crm-padrao'
  AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM "sales_ops_pipeline_stages" x WHERE x."pipeline_id" = p."id" AND x."exit_kind" = e."exit_kind")
ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;--> statement-breakpoint
INSERT INTO "sales_ops_pipeline_transitions" ("org_id", "pipeline_id", "from_stage_id", "to_stage_id")
WITH act AS (
  SELECT s."org_id", s."pipeline_id", s."id", s."type", s."exit_kind", s."position", s."name"
  FROM "sales_ops_pipeline_stages" s
  JOIN "sales_ops_pipelines" p ON p."id" = s."pipeline_id" AND p."system_key" = 'crm-padrao'
  WHERE s."status" = 'active'
    AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM "sales_ops_pipeline_transitions" t WHERE t."pipeline_id" = s."pipeline_id")
), opn AS (
  SELECT a.*, row_number() OVER (PARTITION BY a."pipeline_id" ORDER BY a."position", a."name") AS rn
  FROM act a WHERE a."type" <> 'saida'
)
SELECT "org_id", "pipeline_id", "from_id", "to_id" FROM (
  SELECT a."org_id", a."pipeline_id", a."id" AS "from_id", b."id" AS "to_id" FROM opn a JOIN opn b ON b."pipeline_id" = a."pipeline_id" AND b."rn" = a."rn" + 1
  UNION
  SELECT a."org_id", a."pipeline_id", b."id", a."id" FROM opn a JOIN opn b ON b."pipeline_id" = a."pipeline_id" AND b."rn" = a."rn" + 1
  UNION
  SELECT o."org_id", o."pipeline_id", o."id", e."id" FROM opn o JOIN act e ON e."pipeline_id" = o."pipeline_id" AND e."type" = 'saida'
  UNION
  SELECT e."org_id", e."pipeline_id", e."id", o."id" FROM act e JOIN opn o ON o."pipeline_id" = e."pipeline_id" WHERE e."type" = 'saida' AND e."exit_kind" = 'pausado'
) AS g
ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;--> statement-breakpoint
INSERT INTO "sales_ops_field_definitions" ("org_id", "key", "label", "kind", "reference_target", "multiple", "filterable", "is_system", "removable", "backing_column", "position")
SELECT o."org_id", 'cliente', 'Cliente', 'reference', 'cliente', false, true, true, false, 'client_id', 1
FROM (SELECT "org_id" FROM "sales_ops_pipelines" WHERE "system_key" = 'crm-padrao') AS o
ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;--> statement-breakpoint
INSERT INTO "sales_ops_field_definitions" ("org_id", "key", "label", "kind", "reference_target", "multiple", "filterable", "position")
SELECT DISTINCT lp."org_id", 'produtos', 'Produtos', 'reference', 'produto', true, true, 2
FROM "sales_ops_lead_products" lp
ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;--> statement-breakpoint
INSERT INTO "sales_ops_field_definitions" ("org_id", "key", "label", "kind", "filterable", "position")
SELECT DISTINCT l."org_id", 'descricao', 'Descrição', 'text', false, 3

import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import {
  DEFAULT_PIPELINE_KEY,
  DEFAULT_PIPELINE_NAME,
  DEFAULT_PIPELINE_STAGES,
  DEFAULT_PIPELINE_TRANSITIONS,
} from '@fxl-sales/shared-utils/default-pipeline';
import {
  defaultStageBehavior,
  defaultStageRequiresReason,
  SYSTEM_FIELD_CLIENTE,
} from '@fxl-sales/shared-utils/crm-model';
import { asc, eq } from 'drizzle-orm';
import type { getDb } from '../../db/client.js';
import { salesOpsFieldDefinitions, salesOpsPipelines, salesOpsPipelineStages, salesOpsPipelineTransitions } from '../../db/schema.js';
import { withTenant } from '../sales-ops/leads/with-tenant.js';

type Db = ReturnType<typeof getDb>;

export type PipelineRow = typeof salesOpsPipelines.$inferSelect;
export type FieldDefinitionRow = typeof salesOpsFieldDefinitions.$inferSelect;

function selectPipelines(tx: Db, orgId: string): Promise<PipelineRow[]> {
  return tx
    .select()
    .from(salesOpsPipelines)
    .where(eq(salesOpsPipelines.orgId, orgId))
    .orderBy(asc(salesOpsPipelines.position), asc(salesOpsPipelines.name));
}

function selectFields(tx: Db, orgId: string): Promise<FieldDefinitionRow[]> {
  return tx
    .select()
    .from(salesOpsFieldDefinitions)
    .where(eq(salesOpsFieldDefinitions.orgId, orgId))
    .orderBy(asc(salesOpsFieldDefinitions.position), asc(salesOpsFieldDefinitions.key));
}

/**
 * Seeds the default CRM pipeline (stages and transitions from the shared constant) for an org
 * that has NO pipeline row at all and returns the org's pipelines in order. MUST run inside an
 * already tenant-scoped transaction (see {@link ensureCrmDefaultsForOrg}).
 *
 * "Seeded" means "has ANY pipeline row", never "has the default one": a renamed or archived
 * pipeline is never reinserted. The pipeline INSERT is a bare ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING (absorbs
 * every unique index: org+name, org+system_key, one default per org); a concurrent first caller
 * blocks on it until the winner commits, so the loser's INSERT returns no row and the re-read
 * sees the winner's complete pipeline. Stages and transitions are written only by the caller
 * whose pipeline INSERT returned a row, in the same transaction.
 */
export async function ensureDefaultPipeline(tx: Db, orgId: string): Promise<PipelineRow[]> {
  const existing = await selectPipelines(tx, orgId);
  if (existing.length > 0) return existing;

  const pipelineId = randomUUID();
  const inserted = await tx
    .insert(salesOpsPipelines)
    .values({
      id: pipelineId,
      orgId,
      name: DEFAULT_PIPELINE_NAME,
      systemKey: DEFAULT_PIPELINE_KEY,
      position: 0,
      isDefault: true,
    })
    .onConflictDoNothing()
    .returning({ id: salesOpsPipelines.id });

  if (inserted.length > 0) {
    const stageIds = new Map(DEFAULT_PIPELINE_STAGES.map((stage) => [stage.key as string, randomUUID()] as const));
    await tx.insert(salesOpsPipelineStages).values(
      DEFAULT_PIPELINE_STAGES.map((stage) => ({
        id: stageIds.get(stage.key)!,
        orgId,
        pipelineId,
        name: stage.label,
        position: stage.order,
        type: stage.type,
        behavior: defaultStageBehavior(stage),
        exitKind: stage.exitKind,
        requiresReason: defaultStageRequiresReason(stage),
      })),
    );
    await tx.insert(salesOpsPipelineTransitions).values(
      DEFAULT_PIPELINE_TRANSITIONS.map(([from, to]) => ({
        orgId,
        pipelineId,
        fromStageId: stageIds.get(from)!,
        toStageId: stageIds.get(to)!,
      })),
    );
  }

  return selectPipelines(tx, orgId);
}

/**
 * Ensures the Cliente system field exists. Unlike the pipeline it is re-inserted when missing
 * (it is not removable), keyed by the unique (org_id, key) index.
 */
export async function ensureSystemFieldDefinitions(tx: Db, orgId: string): Promise<FieldDefinitionRow[]> {
  await tx
    .insert(salesOpsFieldDefinitions)
    .values({ orgId, ...SYSTEM_FIELD_CLIENTE })
    .onConflictDoNothing();
  return selectFields(tx, orgId);
}

export async function ensureCrmDefaults(
  tx: Db,
  orgId: string,
): Promise<{ pipelines: PipelineRow[]; fields: FieldDefinitionRow[] }> {
  const pipelines = await ensureDefaultPipeline(tx, orgId);
  const fields = await ensureSystemFieldDefinitions(tx, orgId);
  return { pipelines, fields };
}

/** Opens the tenant transaction and seeds. The public entry point. */
export async function ensureCrmDefaultsForOrg(db: Db, orgId: string) {
  return withTenant(db, orgId, (tx) => ensureCrmDefaults(tx, orgId));
}
/**
 * CRM unificado: the shared vocabulary of the unified lead model (migration 0029).
 *
 * Pure: imports only sibling pure modules, reads no env, no clock, no locale.
 * Open-ended sets (behavior, field kind, reference target, module key, event kind,
 * source) are validated HERE and by zod, never by a database CHECK, because the
 * additive migration guard forbids DROP CONSTRAINT.
 */
import {
  DEFAULT_PIPELINE_STAGES,
  type DefaultPipelineStage,
  type PipelineExitKind,
  type PipelineStageType,
} from './default-pipeline.js';
import { hasCapability, type SalesCapability, type SalesEdition } from './sales-edition.js';

export const CRM_STATUSES = ['active', 'archived'] as const;
export type CrmStatus = (typeof CRM_STATUSES)[number];

export const PIPELINE_STAGE_TYPES = ['normal', 'orcamento', 'saida'] as const satisfies readonly PipelineStageType[];
export const PIPELINE_EXIT_KINDS = ['ganho', 'perdido', 'pausado'] as const satisfies readonly PipelineExitKind[];

export const STAGE_BEHAVIORS = ['none', 'opens_orcamentos'] as const;
export type StageBehavior = (typeof STAGE_BEHAVIORS)[number];

export const FIELD_KINDS = ['text', 'number', 'money', 'date', 'options', 'reference'] as const;
export type FieldKind = (typeof FIELD_KINDS)[number];

export const FIELD_REFERENCE_TARGETS = ['cliente', 'contato', 'produto', 'servico', 'venda'] as const;
export type FieldReferenceTarget = (typeof FIELD_REFERENCE_TARGETS)[number];

export const LEAD_EVENT_KINDS = ['stage_changed'] as const;
export type LeadEventKind = (typeof LEAD_EVENT_KINDS)[number];

export const CRM_MODULE_KEYS = [
  'orcamentos',
  'comissoes',
  'financeiro',
  'finders',
  'catalogo',
  'importacao',
  'clientes',
] as const;
export type CrmModuleKey = (typeof CRM_MODULE_KEYS)[number];

export const CRM_MODULE_SOURCES = ['default', 'token_backfill', 'admin'] as const;
export type CrmModuleSource = (typeof CRM_MODULE_SOURCES)[number];

/**
 * Today's capability -> the module(s) that must ALL be enabled for it.
 * `financeiro` maps only `history` (Geral settings and the archive history) here;
 * phase 3 decides what else the financeiro module gates.
 */
export const CAPABILITY_MODULES: Readonly<Record<SalesCapability, readonly CrmModuleKey[]>> = {
  proposals: ['orcamentos'],
  commissions: ['comissoes'],
  catalog: ['catalogo'],
  clients: ['clientes'],
  import: ['importacao'],
  finders: ['finders'],
  history: ['financeiro'],
  leadFullFields: ['catalogo'],
};

/**
 * The module flags that reproduce an edition exactly: a module is enabled iff every
 * capability mapped to it is granted by the edition. full => all seven;
 * leads => exactly `clientes` and `importacao`. Used by the phase 3 one-time token backfill.
 */
export function modulesForEdition(edition: SalesEdition): Record<CrmModuleKey, boolean> {
  const result = {} as Record<CrmModuleKey, boolean>;
  for (const key of CRM_MODULE_KEYS) {
    const mapped = (Object.keys(CAPABILITY_MODULES) as SalesCapability[]).filter((capability) =>
      CAPABILITY_MODULES[capability].includes(key),
    );
    result[key] = mapped.length > 0 && mapped.every((capability) => hasCapability(edition, capability));
  }
  return result;
}

export function isCrmModuleKey(value: unknown): value is CrmModuleKey {
  return typeof value === 'string' && (CRM_MODULE_KEYS as readonly string[]).includes(value);
}

/** The behavior a default stage is seeded with. */
export function defaultStageBehavior(stage: Pick<DefaultPipelineStage, 'type'>): StageBehavior {
  return stage.type === 'orcamento' ? 'opens_orcamentos' : 'none';
}

/** Only a perdido exit requires a reason. */
export function defaultStageRequiresReason(stage: Pick<DefaultPipelineStage, 'exitKind'>): boolean {
  return stage.exitKind === 'perdido';
}

export interface TransitionPlanStage {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly name: string;
  readonly position: number;
  readonly type: PipelineStageType;
  readonly exitKind: PipelineExitKind | null;
  readonly status: CrmStatus;
}

/**
 * The transition rule migration 0030 applies in SQL, over the ACTIVE stages of one pipeline.
 * open = type normal|orcamento ordered by (position, name); exits = type saida.
 *   open[i] -> open[i+1]; open[i+1] -> open[i]; every open -> every exit;
 *   every exit with exitKind pausado -> every open.
 * Returns unique [fromId, toId] pairs in a stable order.
 */
export function planPipelineTransitions(stages: readonly TransitionPlanStage[]): [string, string][] {
  const active = stages.filter((stage) => stage.status === 'active');
  const byOrder = (a: TransitionPlanStage, b: TransitionPlanStage) =>
    a.position - b.position || (a.name < b.name ? -1 : a.name > b.name ? 1 : 0);
  const open = active.filter((stage) => stage.type !== 'saida').sort(byOrder);
  const exits = active.filter((stage) => stage.type === 'saida').sort(byOrder);
  const pairs = new Map<string, [string, string]>();
  const add = (from: string, to: string) => pairs.set(`${from}>${to}`, [from, to]);
  for (let i = 0; i + 1 < open.length; i += 1) {
    add(open[i]!.id, open[i + 1]!.id);
    add(open[i + 1]!.id, open[i]!.id);
  }
  for (const from of open) for (const to of exits) add(from.id, to.id);
  for (const from of exits) if (from.exitKind === 'pausado') for (const to of open) add(from.id, to.id);
  return [...pairs.values()];
}

/** Key -> stage lookup of the default pipeline, for seeding and parity tests. */
export const DEFAULT_PIPELINE_STAGE_BY_KEY: ReadonlyMap<string, DefaultPipelineStage> = new Map(
  DEFAULT_PIPELINE_STAGES.map((stage) => [stage.key, stage]),
);

  key: 'cliente',
  label: 'Cliente',
  kind: 'reference',
  referenceTarget: 'cliente',
  multiple: false,
  filterable: true,
  isSystem: true,
  removable: false,
  backingColumn: 'client_id',
  position: 1,
} as const;

 ('a0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000010','orgA','Cliente Alfa');

 ('a0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000020','orgA','Produto X',0,0,0);
INSERT INTO sales_ops_sales (id, org_id, sequence, code, client_name_snapshot, seller_name_snapshot, payment_method, condition, base_date, total_brl, seller_commission_pct, finder_commission_pct, tax_pct, net_margin_pct, status) VALUES
 ('a0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000030','orgA',1,'P-1','Cliente Alfa','Vendedora Ana','pix','avista',now(),100000,0,0,0,0,'won'),

 ('a0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000042','orgA','a0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000030','receivable','a0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000040','baixa','2026-10-01',100000,'user_1','Admin');

INSERT INTO sales_ops_lead_stages (id, org_id, name, kind, is_system, position, status, archived_at) VALUES
 ('a0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000101','orgA','Prospecção','normal',false,1,'active',NULL),

 ('a0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000103','orgA','Proposta','conversion',true,3,'active',NULL),

 ('a0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000201','orgA','Lead convertido',NULL,NULL,NULL,'a0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000010','Cliente Alfa',100000,'desc conv','a0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001','Vendedora Ana','a0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000103','2026-09-01T10:00:00Z',0,NULL,'a0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000030',NULL,NULL,NULL),
 ('a0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000202','orgA','Lead perdido','1199','p@x.com','1990-05-04',NULL,'Empresa livre',50000,NULL,'a0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001','Vendedora Ana','a0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000104','2026-09-02T10:00:00Z',0,'Sem orçamento',NULL,NULL,NULL,NULL),
 ('a0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000203','orgA','Lead excluído',NULL,NULL,NULL,NULL,'',0,NULL,NULL,'','a0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000101','2026-09-03T10:00:00Z',0,NULL,NULL,now(),'user_1','Admin'),
 ('a0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000204','orgA','Lead sem vendedor',NULL,'u@x.com',NULL,NULL,'',7000,'',NULL,'','a0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000101','2026-09-04T10:00:00Z',1,NULL,NULL,NULL,NULL,NULL),
 ('a0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000205','orgA','Lead em negociação','1188',NULL,NULL,'a0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000010','Cliente Alfa',12345,NULL,'a0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001','Vendedora Ana','a0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000102','2026-09-05T10:00:00Z',0,NULL,NULL,NULL,NULL,NULL);
INSERT INTO sales_ops_lead_products (id, org_id, lead_id, product_id, product_name_snapshot) VALUES
 ('a0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000301','orgA','a0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000205','a0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000020','Produto X'),
 ('a0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000302','orgA','a0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000205',NULL,'Item livre sem catálogo');
-- orgB: Construbom style, no produtos
INSERT INTO sales_ops_lead_stages (id, org_id, name, kind, is_system, position) VALUES
 ('b0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000101','orgB','Novo','normal',false,1),
 ('b0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000102','orgB','Em negociação','normal',false,2),
 ('b0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000103','orgB','Proposta','conversion',true,3),
 ('b0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000104','orgB','Perdido','lost',true,4),
 ('b0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000105','orgB','Ganho','normal',false,5);
INSERT INTO sales_ops_leads (id, org_id, contact_name, contact_phone, client_name_snapshot, estimated_value_brl, stage_id, position) VALUES
 ('b0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000201','orgB','Lead B1','1177','Construtora Z',900,'b0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000101',0);
-- orgC: person only, no stages
INSERT INTO sales_ops_people (id, org_id, display_name) VALUES ('c0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001','orgC','Pessoa C');
-- legacy finder referral chain (apps, products, finders, links, clicks, conversions) and a finder lead, must NOT be copied
INSERT INTO apps (id, slug, name, publishable_key, secret_key_hash, secret_key_prefix, webhook_signing_secret, allowed_redirect_hosts, status, created_by_user_id) VALUES
 ('d0000000-0000-4000-8000-0000000000a1','app-x','App X','pk_x','h','sk_','w','{example.com}','active','user_1');
INSERT INTO products (id, app_id, slug, name, status) VALUES ('d0000000-0000-4000-8000-0000000000a2','d0000000-0000-4000-8000-0000000000a1','p','P','active');
INSERT INTO finders (id, org_id, status, display_name, contact_email) VALUES ('d0000000-0000-4000-8000-0000000000a3','orgA','approved','Finder','f@x.com');
INSERT INTO referral_links (id, org_id, code, finder_id, app_id, product_id, quoted_setup_brl, quoted_monthly_brl, signature, destination_url) VALUES
 ('d0000000-0000-4000-8000-0000000000a4','orgA','CODE000001','d0000000-0000-4000-8000-0000000000a3','d0000000-0000-4000-8000-0000000000a1','d0000000-0000-4000-8000-0000000000a2',1000,500,'sig','https://example.com/x');
INSERT INTO clicks (click_id, org_id, link_id, finder_id, app_id, product_id) VALUES
 ('CLICK0000000000000000000001','orgA','d0000000-0000-4000-8000-0000000000a4','d0000000-0000-4000-8000-0000000000a3','d0000000-0000-4000-8000-0000000000a1','d0000000-0000-4000-8000-0000000000a2');
INSERT INTO leads (id, org_id, click_id, link_id, status) VALUES ('d0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001','orgA','CLICK0000000000000000000001','d0000000-0000-4000-8000-0000000000a4','converted');
INSERT INTO conversions (source, external_order_id, idempotency_key, org_id, link_id, click_id, finder_id, app_id, product_id, quoted_setup_brl, quoted_monthly_brl, realized_setup_brl, realized_monthly_brl, hold_until, closed_at) VALUES
 ('fxl-financiero','ord1','idem1','orgA','d0000000-0000-4000-8000-0000000000a4','CLICK0000000000000000000001','d0000000-0000-4000-8000-0000000000a3','d0000000-0000-4000-8000-0000000000a1','d0000000-0000-4000-8000-0000000000a2',1000,500,1000,500,now(),now());
`;

type Fingerprint = Record<string, { n: number; hash: string }>;

const L = (suffix: string, org = 'a') => `${org}0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000${suffix}`;

/** Thrown inside a probe transaction to roll it back without reporting a failure. */
const PROBE_ROLLBACK = 'probe-rollback';

describe('migration 0030 on a database populated at 0029', () => {
  let scratch: ScratchDatabase;
  let sourceBefore: Fingerprint;

  /** count + md5 of every row's text form; adminScratch is the superuser, so RLS does not hide rows. */
  async function fingerprint(tables: readonly string[]): Promise<Fingerprint> {
    const result: Fingerprint = {};
    for (const table of tables) {
      const [row] = await scratch.adminScratch.unsafe<{ n: number; hash: string }[]>(
        `SELECT count(*)::int AS n, coalesce(md5(string_agg(t::text, '|' ORDER BY t::text)), '') AS hash FROM ${table} t`,
      );
      result[table] = row!;
    }
    return result;
  }

  function copyChunks(): string[] {
    const sql = fs.readFileSync(path.resolve(MIGRATIONS_FOLDER, FILE_0030), 'utf8');
    return sql.split(BREAKPOINT).map((chunk) => chunk.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  }

  async function rows<T extends object>(query: string): Promise<T[]> {
    return [...(await scratch.adminScratch.unsafe<T[]>(query))];
  }

  beforeAll(async () => {
    scratch = await createScratchDatabase();
    await runDatabaseMigrations({
      databaseUrl: scratch.ownerUrl,
      migrationsFolder: MIGRATIONS_FOLDER,
      throughTag: TAG_0029,
    });
    await scratchClient(scratch).begin(async (tx) => {
      await tx.unsafe(SEED_SQL);
    });
    sourceBefore = await fingerprint(SOURCE_TABLES);
    expect((await fingerprint(CRM_TABLES))['sales_ops_crm_leads']!.n).toBe(0);
    await runDatabaseMigrations({ databaseUrl: scratch.ownerUrl, migrationsFolder: MIGRATIONS_FOLDER });
  }, 120000);

  afterAll(async () => {
    const errors = await cleanupScratch(scratch);
    if (errors.length > 0) throw new AggregateError(errors, 'scratch database cleanup failed');
  });

  it('0030 is additive only (no DROP, DELETE, TRUNCATE, RENAME, GRANT, ROLE) and never writes a source table', () => {
    const sql = fs.readFileSync(path.resolve(MIGRATIONS_FOLDER, FILE_0030), 'utf8');
    expect(scanSql(sql)).toEqual([]);
    expect(sql).not.toMatch(/\bUPDATE\b/i);
    for (const chunk of copyChunks().slice(1)) {
      expect(chunk, chunk.slice(0, 60)).toMatch(/^INSERT INTO "sales_ops_(pipelines|pipeline_stages|pipeline_transitions|field_definitions|crm_leads|crm_lead_field_values|crm_lead_events)"/);
    }
  });

  it('every source table (19) is identical before and after (row count and content hash)', async () => {
    expect(await fingerprint(SOURCE_TABLES)).toEqual(sourceBefore);
    // spot checks that the fixture was not empty
    expect(sourceBefore['sales_ops_leads']!.n).toBe(6);
    expect(sourceBefore['sales_ops_lead_products']!.n).toBe(2);
    expect(sourceBefore['sales_ops_settlements']!.n).toBe(1);
    expect(sourceBefore['integration_outbox']!.n).toBe(1);
    expect(sourceBefore['leads']!.n).toBe(1);
    expect(sourceBefore['conversions']!.n).toBe(1);
  });

  it('payables, receivables, settlements and the outbox are unchanged and not touched by the new model', async () => {
    const [counts] = await rows<{ r: number; p: number; s: number; o: number }>(
      `SELECT (SELECT count(*) FROM sales_ops_receivables)::int r, (SELECT count(*) FROM sales_ops_payables)::int p,
              (SELECT count(*) FROM sales_ops_settlements)::int s, (SELECT count(*) FROM integration_outbox)::int o`,
    );
    expect(counts).toEqual({ r: 1, p: 1, s: 1, o: 1 });
  });

  it('copies every lead with the SAME id; counts and the estimated value sum match the source per org', async () => {
    const source = await rows<{ org_id: string; n: number; total: number }>(
      `SELECT org_id, count(*)::int n, sum(estimated_value_brl)::int total FROM sales_ops_leads GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 1`,
    );
    const target = await rows<{ org_id: string; n: number; total: number }>(
      `SELECT org_id, count(*)::int n, sum(estimated_value_brl)::int total FROM sales_ops_crm_leads GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 1`,
    );
    expect(target).toEqual(source);
    expect(source.find((r) => r.org_id === 'orgA')).toEqual({ org_id: 'orgA', n: 5, total: 169345 });
    const diff = await rows<{ id: string }>(
      `SELECT id FROM sales_ops_leads EXCEPT SELECT id FROM sales_ops_crm_leads`,
    );
    expect(diff).toEqual([]);
    const legacy = await rows<{ id: string }>(`SELECT id FROM sales_ops_crm_leads WHERE id = 'd0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001'`);
    expect(legacy).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('copies each lead column for column, including soft delete, unassigned and the proposta link', async () => {
    const lead = async (id: string) =>
      (await rows<Record<string, unknown>>(`SELECT * FROM sales_ops_crm_leads WHERE id = '${id}'`))[0]!;
    const sourceLead = async (id: string) =>
      (await rows<Record<string, unknown>>(`SELECT * FROM sales_ops_leads WHERE id = '${id}'`))[0]!;
    const shared = [
      'org_id', 'contact_name', 'contact_phone', 'contact_email', 'stage_id', 'position', 'stage_changed_at',
      'seller_person_id', 'seller_name_snapshot', 'client_id', 'client_name_snapshot', 'estimated_value_brl',
      'lost_reason', 'sale_id', 'created_at', 'updated_at', 'deleted_at', 'deleted_by_user_id', 'deleted_by_name',
    ];
    for (const id of ['201', '202', '203', '204', '205'].map((s) => L(s)).concat([L('201', 'b')])) {
      const copied = await lead(id);
      const original = await sourceLead(id);
      for (const column of shared) expect(copied[column], `${id}.${column}`).toEqual(original[column]);
    }
    expect((await lead(L('201')))['sale_id']).toBe(L('030'));
    expect((await lead(L('203')))['deleted_by_name']).toBe('Admin');
    expect((await lead(L('204')))['seller_person_id']).toBeNull();
    expect((await lead(L('202')))['client_id']).toBeNull();
    expect((await lead(L('202')))['client_name_snapshot']).toBe('Empresa livre');
    expect((await lead(L('201')))['client_id']).toBe(L('010'));
  });

  it('keeps at most one lead per proposta and every lead stage inside its own pipeline', async () => {
    const [orphans] = await rows<{ n: number }>(
      `SELECT count(*)::int n FROM sales_ops_crm_leads l
       LEFT JOIN sales_ops_pipeline_stages s ON s.org_id = l.org_id AND s.pipeline_id = l.pipeline_id AND s.id = l.stage_id
       WHERE s.id IS NULL`,
    );
    expect(orphans!.n).toBe(0);
    await expect(
      scratch.adminScratch.unsafe(
        `INSERT INTO sales_ops_crm_leads (org_id, contact_name, pipeline_id, stage_id, sale_id)
         SELECT org_id, 'dup', pipeline_id, stage_id, sale_id FROM sales_ops_crm_leads WHERE id = '${L('201')}'`,
      ),
    ).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: '23505', constraint_name: 'sales_ops_crm_leads_org_sale_idx' });
  });

  it('migrates the etapas of an org with its OWN ids, names and positions, and adds Ganho and Pausado', async () => {
    const stages = await rows<{ id: string; name: string; position: number; type: string; behavior: string; exit_kind: string | null; requires_reason: boolean; status: string }>(
      `SELECT id, name, position, type, behavior, exit_kind, requires_reason, status FROM sales_ops_pipeline_stages WHERE org_id = 'orgA' ORDER BY position`,
    );
    expect(stages.map((s) => [s.name, s.position, s.type, s.behavior, s.exit_kind, s.requires_reason, s.status])).toEqual([
      ['Prospecção', 1, 'normal', 'none', null, false, 'active'],
      ['Em negociação', 2, 'normal', 'none', null, false, 'active'],
      ['Proposta', 3, 'orcamento', 'opens_orcamentos', null, false, 'active'],
      ['Perdido', 4, 'saida', 'none', 'perdido', true, 'active'],
      ['Antiga', 5, 'normal', 'none', null, false, 'archived'],
      ['Ganho', 6, 'saida', 'none', 'ganho', false, 'active'],
      ['Pausado', 7, 'saida', 'none', 'pausado', false, 'active'],
    ]);
    expect(stages.slice(0, 5).map((s) => s.id)).toEqual(['101', '102', '103', '104', '105'].map((s) => L(s)));
    const noRename = await rows<{ id: string }>(
      `SELECT id FROM sales_ops_lead_stages EXCEPT SELECT id FROM sales_ops_pipeline_stages`,
    );
    expect(noRename).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('never loses an exit when an org already named a normal etapa Ganho', async () => {
    const names = await rows<{ name: string; type: string; exit_kind: string | null }>(
      `SELECT name, type, exit_kind FROM sales_ops_pipeline_stages WHERE org_id = 'orgB' AND name LIKE 'Ganho%' ORDER BY name`,
    );
    expect(names).toEqual([
      { name: 'Ganho', type: 'normal', exit_kind: null },
      { name: 'Ganho (saída)', type: 'saida', exit_kind: 'ganho' },
    ]);
  });

  it('gives an org with no etapa the pure default pipeline, equal to DEFAULT_PIPELINE_STAGES and its transitions', async () => {
    const stages = await rows<{ id: string; name: string; position: number; type: string; behavior: string; exit_kind: string | null; requires_reason: boolean }>(
      `SELECT id, name, position, type, behavior, exit_kind, requires_reason FROM sales_ops_pipeline_stages WHERE org_id = 'orgC' ORDER BY position`,
    );
    expect(stages.map((s) => [s.name, s.position, s.type, s.behavior, s.exit_kind, s.requires_reason])).toEqual(
      DEFAULT_PIPELINE_STAGES.map((s) => [s.label, s.order, s.type, defaultStageBehavior(s), s.exitKind, defaultStageRequiresReason(s)]),
    );
    const idByKey = new Map(DEFAULT_PIPELINE_STAGES.map((s, i) => [s.key as string, stages[i]!.id]));
    const transitions = await rows<{ from_stage_id: string; to_stage_id: string }>(
      `SELECT from_stage_id, to_stage_id FROM sales_ops_pipeline_transitions WHERE org_id = 'orgC'`,
    );
    expect(new Set(transitions.map((t) => `${t.from_stage_id}>${t.to_stage_id}`))).toEqual(
      new Set(DEFAULT_PIPELINE_TRANSITIONS.map(([a, b]) => `${idByKey.get(a)}>${idByKey.get(b)}`)),
    );
  });

  it('the SQL transition graph equals the TS rule planPipelineTransitions for every org', async () => {
    for (const org of ['orgA', 'orgB', 'orgC']) {
      const stages = await rows<{ id: string; name: string; position: number; type: TransitionPlanStage['type']; exit_kind: TransitionPlanStage['exitKind']; status: TransitionPlanStage['status'] }>(
        `SELECT id, name, position, type, exit_kind, status FROM sales_ops_pipeline_stages WHERE org_id = '${org}'`,
      );
      const planned = planPipelineTransitions(stages.map((s) => ({ id: s.id, name: s.name, position: s.position, type: s.type, exitKind: s.exit_kind, status: s.status })));
      const stored = await rows<{ from_stage_id: string; to_stage_id: string }>(
        `SELECT from_stage_id, to_stage_id FROM sales_ops_pipeline_transitions WHERE org_id = '${org}'`,
      );
      expect(new Set(stored.map((t) => `${t.from_stage_id}>${t.to_stage_id}`)), org).toEqual(new Set(planned.map(([a, b]) => `${a}>${b}`)));
    }
    const [a] = await rows<{ n: number }>(`SELECT count(*)::int n FROM sales_ops_pipeline_transitions WHERE org_id = 'orgA'`);
    expect(a!.n).toBe(16);
  });

  it('creates one default pipeline per org, named and keyed, and no module rows (the token is not in the database)', async () => {
    const pipelines = await rows<{ org_id: string; name: string; system_key: string; is_default: boolean }>(
      `SELECT org_id, name, system_key, is_default FROM sales_ops_pipelines ORDER BY org_id`,
    );
    expect(pipelines).toEqual(
      ['orgA', 'orgB', 'orgC'].map((org_id) => ({ org_id, name: 'Funil CRM padrão', system_key: 'crm-padrao', is_default: true })),
    );
    expect((await fingerprint(['sales_ops_org_modules']))['sales_ops_org_modules']!.n).toBe(0);
  });

  it('Cliente becomes the non-removable system field; other fields exist only where data needs them', async () => {
    const fields = await rows<{ org_id: string; key: string; kind: string; reference_target: string | null; multiple: boolean; is_system: boolean; removable: boolean; backing_column: string | null }>(
      `SELECT org_id, key, kind, reference_target, multiple, is_system, removable, backing_column FROM sales_ops_field_definitions ORDER BY org_id, position`,
    );
    const cliente = { key: 'cliente', kind: 'reference', reference_target: 'cliente', multiple: false, is_system: true, removable: false, backing_column: 'client_id' };
    expect(fields.filter((f) => f.org_id === 'orgA').map((f) => f.key)).toEqual(['cliente', 'produtos', 'descricao', 'nascimento']);
    expect(fields.filter((f) => f.org_id === 'orgB').map((f) => f.key)).toEqual(['cliente']);
    expect(fields.filter((f) => f.org_id === 'orgC').map((f) => f.key)).toEqual(['cliente']);
    for (const org of ['orgA', 'orgB', 'orgC']) {
      expect(fields.find((f) => f.org_id === org && f.key === 'cliente')).toMatchObject({ org_id: org, ...cliente });
    }
    expect(fields.find((f) => f.key === 'produtos')).toMatchObject({ kind: 'reference', reference_target: 'produto', multiple: true, is_system: false, removable: true });
  });

  it('lead produtos become Produto values, FREE items (no catalog row) included with their name snapshot', async () => {
    const values = await rows<{ lead_id: string; position: number; value_product_id: string | null; value_text: string | null; ref_name_snapshot: string }>(
      `SELECT v.lead_id, v.position, v.value_product_id, v.value_text, v.ref_name_snapshot
       FROM sales_ops_crm_lead_field_values v JOIN sales_ops_field_definitions f ON f.id = v.field_id AND f.key = 'produtos'
       ORDER BY v.lead_id, v.position`,
    );
    expect(values).toEqual([
      { lead_id: L('205'), position: 0, value_product_id: L('020'), value_text: null, ref_name_snapshot: 'Produto X' },
      { lead_id: L('205'), position: 1, value_product_id: null, value_text: 'Item livre sem catálogo', ref_name_snapshot: 'Item livre sem catálogo' },
    ]);
    const [counts] = await rows<{ source: number }>(`SELECT count(*)::int source FROM sales_ops_lead_products`);
    expect(values).toHaveLength(counts!.source);
  });

  it('description and birth date become typed values; an empty description is preserved', async () => {
    const desc = await rows<{ lead_id: string; value_text: string }>(
      `SELECT v.lead_id, v.value_text FROM sales_ops_crm_lead_field_values v JOIN sales_ops_field_definitions f ON f.id = v.field_id AND f.key = 'descricao' ORDER BY v.lead_id`,
    );
    expect(desc).toEqual([{ lead_id: L('201'), value_text: 'desc conv' }, { lead_id: L('204'), value_text: '' }]);
    const birth = await rows<{ lead_id: string; value_date: string }>(
      `SELECT v.lead_id, v.value_date::text FROM sales_ops_crm_lead_field_values v JOIN sales_ops_field_definitions f ON f.id = v.field_id AND f.key = 'nascimento'`,
    );
    expect(birth).toEqual([{ lead_id: L('202'), value_date: '1990-05-04' }]);
  });

  it('stage_changed_at becomes the first and only history event of each lead', async () => {
    const events = await rows<{ lead_id: string; kind: string; from_stage_id: string | null; to_stage_id: string; reason: string | null; actor_user_id: string; actor_name_snapshot: string; same_time: boolean }>(
      `SELECT e.lead_id, e.kind, e.from_stage_id, e.to_stage_id, e.reason, e.actor_user_id, e.actor_name_snapshot,
              e.occurred_at = l.stage_changed_at AS same_time
       FROM sales_ops_crm_lead_events e JOIN sales_ops_leads l ON l.id = e.lead_id ORDER BY e.lead_id`,
    );
    expect(events).toHaveLength(6);
    for (const event of events) {
      expect(event).toMatchObject({ kind: 'stage_changed', from_stage_id: null, actor_user_id: 'system', actor_name_snapshot: 'Migração', same_time: true });
    }
    expect(events.find((e) => e.lead_id === L('202'))).toMatchObject({ to_stage_id: L('104'), reason: 'Sem orçamento' });
  });

  it('leaves no orphan foreign key anywhere in the new tables', async () => {
    const fks = await rows<{ conrelid: string; conname: string; validated: boolean }>(
      `SELECT conrelid::regclass::text conrelid, conname, convalidated validated FROM pg_constraint
       WHERE contype = 'f' AND conrelid::regclass::text = ANY(ARRAY[${CRM_TABLES.map((t) => `'${t}'`).join(',')}])`,
    );
    expect(fks.length).toBe(20);
    expect(fks.filter((fk) => !fk.validated)).toEqual([]);
    const [orphans] = await rows<{ n: number }>(
      `SELECT ((SELECT count(*) FROM sales_ops_crm_lead_field_values v LEFT JOIN sales_ops_crm_leads l ON l.org_id = v.org_id AND l.id = v.lead_id WHERE l.id IS NULL)
             + (SELECT count(*) FROM sales_ops_crm_lead_events e LEFT JOIN sales_ops_crm_leads l ON l.org_id = e.org_id AND l.id = e.lead_id WHERE l.id IS NULL)
             + (SELECT count(*) FROM sales_ops_pipeline_transitions t LEFT JOIN sales_ops_pipeline_stages s ON s.org_id = t.org_id AND s.pipeline_id = t.pipeline_id AND s.id = t.from_stage_id WHERE s.id IS NULL))::int n`,
    );
    expect(orphans!.n).toBe(0);
  });

  it('RLS isolates orgs on every new table, for the owner role that FORCE RLS applies to', async () => {
    const client = scratchClient(scratch);
    for (const table of CRM_TABLES) {
      const counts = await client.begin(async (tx) => {
        const none = (await tx.unsafe(`SELECT count(*)::int n FROM ${table}`))[0]!.n as number;
        await tx.unsafe(`SELECT set_config('app.current_org_id', 'orgA', true)`);
        const a = (await tx.unsafe(`SELECT count(*)::int n FROM ${table} WHERE org_id <> 'orgA'`))[0]!.n as number;
        const own = (await tx.unsafe(`SELECT count(*)::int n FROM ${table}`))[0]!.n as number;
        return { none, a, own };
      });
      expect(counts.none, `${table} without a tenant`).toBe(0);
      expect(counts.a, `${table} foreign rows visible to orgA`).toBe(0);
      const total = (await fingerprint([table]))[table]!.n;
      if (total > 0) expect(counts.own, `${table} own rows`).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    }
    await expect(
      client.begin(async (tx) => {
        await tx.unsafe(`SELECT set_config('app.current_org_id', 'orgB', true)`);
        await tx.unsafe(`UPDATE sales_ops_crm_leads SET contact_name = 'x' WHERE org_id = 'orgA'`).then((r) => {
          if (r.count !== 0) throw new Error(`orgB updated ${r.count} orgA rows`);
        });
      }),
    ).resolves.toBeUndefined();
  });

  /**
   * The purge helper of the integration suite deletes the sale tree after its lead rows. A CRM
   * lead copy restrict-references the same sale, so the helper's sequence fails with 23503 unless
   * the CRM rows of the org are deleted first. Each attempt runs in a transaction that is rolled back.
   */
  async function purgeSequenceOutcome(org: string, withCrmDeletes: boolean): Promise<string | 'ok'> {
    const sales = ['sales_ops_lead_products', 'sales_ops_leads', 'sales_ops_payables', 'sales_ops_receivables', 'sales_ops_sale_professionals', 'sales_ops_sale_items', 'sales_ops_sales'];
    const crm = ['sales_ops_crm_lead_notes', 'sales_ops_crm_lead_followups', 'sales_ops_crm_lead_field_values', 'sales_ops_crm_lead_events', 'sales_ops_crm_leads'];
    try {
      await scratch.adminScratch.begin(async (tx) => {
        await tx.unsafe(`SET LOCAL session_replication_role = replica`);
        await tx.unsafe(`DELETE FROM sales_ops_settlements WHERE org_id = '${org}'`);
        await tx.unsafe(`SET LOCAL session_replication_role = origin`);
        if (withCrmDeletes) for (const table of crm) await tx.unsafe(`DELETE FROM ${table} WHERE org_id = '${org}'`);
        for (const table of sales) await tx.unsafe(`DELETE FROM ${table} WHERE org_id = '${org}'`);
        throw new Error(PROBE_ROLLBACK);
      });
      return 'ok';
    } catch (error) {
      if (error instanceof Error && error.message === PROBE_ROLLBACK) return 'ok';
      return String((error as { code?: string }).code ?? error);
    }
  }

  it('the purge helper sequence fails with 23503 without the CRM deletes and succeeds with them', async () => {
    expect(await purgeSequenceOutcome('orgA', false)).toBe('23503');
    expect(await purgeSequenceOutcome('orgA', true)).toBe('ok');
  });

  it('running the backfill SQL a second time is a no-op for every destination and source table', async () => {
    const before = await fingerprint([...CRM_TABLES, ...SOURCE_TABLES]);
    await scratchClient(scratch).begin(async (tx) => {
      for (const chunk of copyChunks()) await tx.unsafe(chunk);
    });
    expect(await fingerprint([...CRM_TABLES, ...SOURCE_TABLES])).toEqual(before);
  });
});
